# M4 plan: fights and loot

The plan of record for [m4-fights-and-loot.md](m4-fights-and-loot.md) (the spec: goal, DoD, research). Section
numbers are stable: append, never renumber, so briefs can cite "§4.2". The process (§6) is M3's (m3-plan.md §6,
P1-P8, with D38's model mix): one fresh Opus 5.5 lead session per item at high effort, Sonnet 5.5 slices under 300K of
context in their own worktrees, Haiku 5.5 merges and doc passes, one Opus review per item, the live gate by the lead
alone, frames judged solo (M3 D42), a commit per slice through `tools/commit.ps1`. The rules are CLAUDE.md, "Hard
rules" and "Rules for agent sessions"; a brief cites them and restates nothing. M3's decisions are cited as "M3 D<n>"
(m3-plan.md §7); this plan's own are D1-D25 (§7).

## 3. The waves

A wave is two or three items with a theme and a checkpoint; an item is one fresh lead session; a slice is one Sonnet
agent. Estimates are shares of a week on the owner's meter (1% is about $22.5), item and lead together, sized from
M3's as-run shares (m3-plan.md §8: 1.2 7%, 2.1 2%, 2.2+2.3 4%, 3.1 2%, 3.2 5%, 4.1 3%, 4.2 7%; about 37% against a
48% estimate): a wire-and-loop item that needs live rounds against ACE is 5-6%, a panel item 3%, a mechanical item 2-4%.

| Wave | Item | Theme and content | Gate | Share |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1.1 | **The loop's remainder and the fighter:** the fighter's dat pins and template, "Holtburg Hunter" in the mod's protected list (one install), `--scenario=fighter-build`; the loop rows DoD 1 keeps (the live legs' contended rule, the shader cache under `-Parallel`, the UI-step GC margin, `summary.json`, `restore --locked-mode`, the test-count baseline, `frame_over_50` surfaced by smoke, the `[net]` account-name line, the traits of D8), the M2 soak run | loopback `fighter=ready level=40 template=ok`; the mod installed; the soak run's CRC and duplicate counts printed | 3% |
| 1 | 1.2 | **Melee and missile on the Action API:** the ported codecs (0x0008, 0x000A, 0x01B7; 01A7, 01B8), the attack state, `Attack`, `MissileAttack`, `CancelAttack` and their trackers, the stand-in's swing and shot in ACE's order, `HuntGround` rows, `--scenario=hunt` in its first form (one kill on the Low B belt, back to Holtburg), the `hunt` leg and `login-hunt` | loopback `MeleeLiveTests`, `MissileLiveTests`, `HuntLiveTests`; the `hunt` leg judged `ok=true kills>=1 damage_dealt>0` (the fields exist in M3's shape), `swings>=1 shots>=1 hits>=1` report-only | 6% |
| 1 | 1.3 | **Targeting and the combat UI:** the health meter on the 01C0 pushes, the combat window's buttons send `Attack`, the power bar driven by the action, the weapon kinds, the auto-target nits, `attack_frame_max_ms` (report-only), frames `c1-combat`, `c1-powerbar` of a real attack | client tests, play-checks, the frames; live, the target's meter lower than at selection | 3% |
| — | checkpoint 1 | **"The fighter fights."** The 1.3 lead reports the spend and the state and pings the owner (D3) | | |
| 2 | 2.1 | **Containers and item moves:** 0196, 0052 decoded and 0195 sent; `OpenContainer`, `CloseContainer` and their trackers; `ViewContents` into `SidePacks`; `PickUp` (from the ground and an open container), `Drop`, the three splits, `Give` and their trackers; the stand-in's corpse with contents; the Give NPC found by SELECT | loopback `ContainerLiveTests` (a side pack at login, the fighter's own kill's corpse, one chest), `ItemMovesLiveTests` on Lifestone Throwaway; `corpses_opened>=1` | 5% |
| 2 | 2.2 | **Loot in the loops and the inventory:** `LootRules`, the hunt loop loots its kills, the death loop recovers the mage's corpse (D5), the inventory and backpack panels with side-pack contents, the container window, the minimal drag (D4), `loot_frame_max_ms` (report-only), a corpse frame | leg `hunt`: `looted>=1`; `bsd-death`: `corpse_looted=` report-only; frames | 5% |
| — | checkpoint 2 | **"The fighter kills and loots."** The 2.2 lead reports and pings (D3) | | |
| 3 | 3.1 | **Recovery and the hunt loop:** `UseWithTarget` and the kit tracker, potions by `Use` with their tracker, the recovery rule, the full hunt loop (kills until a count or a time, heal, loot, return), the `hunt` leg judged `looted>=1 heals>=1`, `attack_frame_max_ms` and `loot_frame_max_ms` judged; **added at checkpoint 2:** the death loop's new direction (D20: decoy items, re-gear, re-buff, the fight at the den, then the corpse trip; slices d0-d2 of §4.6) and the polish slice (D21) | loopback `HealLiveTests` (the fighter after hits; the mage's 0x04FC), `HuntLiveTests` in full, `DeathLiveTests` with the decoys; smoke `login-hunt`, `login-death` | 5%, plus about 1.5% for D20 (D22) and 1% for D21 funded by wave 2's unspent share |
| 3 | 3.2 | **Vendors and appraisal:** 0062 into `VendorStock`, `Buy`, `Sell`, `Identify` and 00C9 into `Appraisal`, the trackers, the sell at the loop's end, the vendor panel's buy and sell, the examine panel (D6), `vendor_frame_max_ms` (report-only) | loopback `VendorLiveTests` (one Handy Healing Kit bought at the predicted price; the hunt's loot sold), `AppraiseLiveTests`; `sold>=1 pyreals=<delta>` report-only | 5% |
| — | checkpoint 3 | **"The fighter heals, sells and buys."** The 3.2 lead reports and pings (D3) | | |

| | | **Build** | | **38%** |

Build 38% plus about 2% of planning: **40% against the 45% stop line (D2)**. The margin is 5%, so a lead asks before
exceeding its item's share by 20% (M3 D5), and each checkpoint re-estimates the waves left against what remains. The
dollar figure, once: 45% is about $1,010 at $22.5 per 1%.

Order, all serial: 1.1 → 1.2 → 1.3 (1.2 needs the fighter; 1.3 needs the attack action). Wave 2: 2.1 then 2.2.
Wave 3: 3.1 then 3.2 (the sell at the loop's end is 3.2's; 3.1 ends its loop at Holtburg without it). Wave 4: 4.1
then 4.2. Each later wave is sliced by its first lead at the wave boundary (M3 P8), in the shape of §4, with at most
four owner questions in one call. Each wave's first slice reads its rows of docs/research/openac-m4-map.md §1 before
the wire, and the wave's gate runs the §2 checks of that wave's rows.

## 4. Wave 1's slice tables

Every slice: a fresh Sonnet 5.5 agent, 60-120 calls, under 300K of context, in its own worktree cut from the item
branch `m4/<item>` (`.claude/worktrees/m4-<item>-<slice>`, `git config core.longpaths true`); at most four files to
create and eight to edit; one acceptance command (`slice-check.ps1 -MinTests <count> -Steps <steps> -Rerun`), one
Godot step at most; the handoff file `docs/handoff-m4-<item>.md` in the branch (removed before the merge). A new judge
or verdict field is report-only in the slice that adds it. The test baseline is the count `slice-check.ps1` reports on
master at the item's start (7,965 default and 76 Exhaustive at M3's end). **Amended by D28 (coverage, not count):** the count is a floor that only the lead lowers, for deletions it names with where their behaviour is proved. **Amended by the polish pass (D30 D, review P-R4):** the tests that read the retail dats run only in the Exhaustive tier, so a slice that touches `src/Lifestone.Data`, or one of the Client folders whose tests moved there (`src/Lifestone.Client/` `Audio`, `Effects`, `Interiors`, `Movement`, `Navigation`, `Objects`, `Play`, `PortalSpace`, `Rendering`, `Sky`), runs `slice-check.ps1 -Exhaustive` as well (its acceptance line gains `-Exhaustive`); the default run no longer reads those tests, so skipping the flag hides a break until the item's G-A. "Read" lists at most five pointers beyond
CLAUDE.md's rules section and the slice's own row. Rounds of one agent stay under 300K: an agent that needs a second
round ends with its handoff and a fresh agent continues (M3 §8: p5a, p6 and h ran over).

### 4.1 Item 1.1 The loop's remainder and the fighter (branch `m4/1-1`)

Precondition: the backend down; D1, D8 stand. **Research first (the lead, read-only, with the backend up for the
SELECTs only):** the fighter's kit by wcid from the world database (a one-handed melee weapon the Heavy Weapons skill
uses and that Renald the Elder buys back, a bow, arrows, the leather set BSD Mage's `GearKit` already names, Handy
Healing Kit 628, Potion of Healing 377), the creation skills the 11.6096 CharGen offers (expected: Heavy Weapons,
Missile Weapons, Healing, Melee Defense), and the Low B belt's generator cells against the pinned release (spec §5.9).
The lead writes the wcids and cells into the s0 and 1.2 s4 briefs. Slices: s0 first; s1 and s3 as lanes; then s2 and
s4 as lanes; s5 last.

| Slice | Scope | Files (create / edit) | Read | Acceptance |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| s0 the fighter's dat pins and template | `FighterTemplate` (the creation intent of spec §5.10: Aluvian, Strength, Endurance and Coordination high; one melee weapon skill specialised, Missile Weapons, Healing and Melee Defense trained; the spending plan to level 40 from the XpTable: attributes to the template's values, the trained skills by priority, leftovers forward) as data in `Session/Fighter/`; a dat test pinning, for the Aluvian heritage, the costs of the template's skills by `CharGenData.GetSkillTrainedCost` (as M3 1.1 s0 did) and `XpTable.Levels[40]`; `FighterKit` (the wcids the lead's SELECT gave, with burdens) | create `src/Lifestone.Client/Session/Fighter/FighterTemplate.cs`, `Session/Fighter/FighterKit.cs`, `tests/Lifestone.Data.Tests/CharGen/FighterTemplateDatTests.cs`, `tests/Lifestone.Client.Tests/Fighter/FighterKitTests.cs` | spec §5.10; `Session/Mage/MageTemplate.cs`, `GearKit.cs`; `XpTableData.cs`; docs/session.md §29 (1504) | `slice-check -MinTests <base>`; the pinned costs and XP in the handoff: s2 depends on them |

monster corpse (`W/Corpse.cs` at 95ef426d, D5) and the shard's corpse properties by a read-only SELECT; Renald the
Elder 718's buy type mask (the loot rule's ItemType filter); a capture of one loot move from the hunter's own kill's

files: the hunt, the death loop, the panels); then s3; s4 last. 2.2's lead runs checkpoint 2 (D3) after the merge.

### 4.6 Item 3.1 Recovery and the hunt loop (branch `m4/3-1`; wave 3's first item, sliced by its lead at fada7a3)

The shape and rules of §4 hold. **Slice size (2.2's lesson, §8):** every code slice of 2.2 ran 129-218 calls with seven-item
briefs; a 3.1 slice has four or five items with their tests, and its brief says so. Precondition: 2.2 merged, the backend down,
checkpoint 2 answered (§0, D20, D21). **Research (the lead, 2026-10-21; `cache/m4/3-1/`; read-only SELECTs with MariaDB
alone, then stopped; ACE 95ef426d under `vendor-eval/ACE`):**

- *Kits and potions.* Handy Healing Kit 628: Healer (WeenieType 28), ItemType 0x80, burden 50, Value 10, BoosterEnum 2
  (Health), BoostValue 50 (PropertyInt 90; `FighterKit`'s "boost 16" is wrong, s0 corrects it), 20 uses (MaxStructure 91 and
  Structure 92), HealkitMod 1.0 (PropertyFloat 100); the kit is consumed at 0 uses. Adept Healing Kit 629: 25 uses, boost 75,
  Value 50. Potion of Healing 377: Food (WeenieType 18), ItemType 0x80, burden 15, Value 170, stack 100, BoosterEnum 2,
  BoostValue 25 (Stamina 378 and Mana 379: enums 4 and 6). ACE's `Healer.DoHealing` (`W/Healer.cs`): the heal is a random
  amount from a band of Healing skill x HealkitMod, doubled on a critical, capped at the missing vital, and **capped again by
  stamina** (the cost is a fifth of the heal; with too little stamina the heal is stamina x 5), so a kit's heal depends on
  both vitals. The capture reads the live numbers; the `ItemType` of kits and potions (0x80) closes `TODO(m4-kit-itemtype)`.
- *The forest (D12, closes `m4-forest-cells` and the forest half of `m4-creature-level`).* Generator 21183's tree to six levels
  (`forest-creatures.tsv`: 40 creatures, levels 3-171 with the depth-3 specials) and its spots (`forest-cells.tsv`: **6,618
  spots world-wide**, not the 82 of spec §5.9; `forest-candidates.tsv`: the 60 nearest Holtburg). The nearest strip is the
  0x9FB* landblocks, 7.7-9.2 map units west (4-9 spots a landblock); ACE rolls a spot's children by cumulative probability, so a
  cell may hold nothing (the hunt tries the next cell, as on the belt). The hunter is level 40: the forest row's band is **30-40**
  (Tusker Crimsonback, Goldenback Tusker, Wild and Crude Monouga, Shadow, Olthoi Nymph at 30; Copper Golem, Copper Golem Kingpin,
  Drudge Lurker, Olthoi Drone, Nasty Scarecrow, Gotrok Lithos, Skeleton Lord at 40), `Tusker Shrine Statue` (171) and Fisana the
  Jeweler (an NPC) are never targets by the band; the belt stays the default ground (`--hunt-ground belt|forest`).
- *The xp row.* `xp_gained` with `kills=0` (+189, +170, +128 at the first evade, hit and resist) is ACE's skill-use experience
  (`Entity/Proficiency.cs`, read by a lookup, `cache/m4/3-1/xp-rule.md`): on a successful use of a trained skill ACE grants
  round(1.1 x the other party's skill) once per rise in difficulty or per 15 minutes (a hit: the target's defence; an evade: the
  attacker's attack skill; a resist: the caster's skill); +189, +170 and +128 are 1.1 x 172, 155 and 116. It is not kill
  experience and the hunt's `xp_gained` stays the total-experience delta (closes the §9 row).
- *The death loop (D20).* ACE's drop rule, read in `Entity/DeathItem.cs` and `W/Player_Death.cs` 481-603, 629-673: the
  candidates are everything the player carries except pyreals, **bonded items** and (below level 35) wielded items; the category is
  the ItemType (`DeathItem.GetCategory`: **Container is None, so packs never drop**); a stack counts at its per-unit value and drops
  one unit; the list is sorted by category then value, every item after the first of its category is halved, then +-10% variance,
  and the top N drop with N = level/20 + rand(0..2), at most 14 (**BSD Mage, level 126, drops 6 to 8**; none up to level 10).
  **What must be protected is what the loops need to kill the tuskers** (the owner's point, D22). By the SELECTs and a spell-table
  lookup (`cache/m4/3-1/scarab-table.md`): the Greater Amuli Shadow Coat 6600 and Leggings 6606 and the **Platinum Scarab 8897 are
  bonded and never drop** (the 2.2 corpse listings hold none of them); the buff cycle and the fight cast at level VII (the
  Platinum Scarab; Focus and Willpower first at VI on the Pyreal Scarab), and **the Mana Scarab (15,000 each, the most valuable
  item the mage carries) is used only by level VIII spells, which no loop casts at its default settings**, so it is an item the
  mage does not need. The droppable items the loops do need rank at most at **The Healer's Heart 5,400** (the wand; worst case
  5,940), then the Olthoi Helm 2,200, Platemail Gauntlets 1,600, Sollerets 653 and the components (Pyreal Scarab 1,000, the rest
  lower); the leather set in the pack (Leggings 2,400) is not needed. So the wand survives whenever at least N items rank above
  it in every draw: the unneeded Mana Scarab is one of them and the decoys are the rest. **Eight decoys, ordinary light items in
  eight different categories, each the first of its category and worth at least 8,000 (worst case 7,200 against the wand's
  5,940; `decoys.tsv`):** Massive Mana Charge 27329 (ManaStone, 65,000, burden 50; sold by 11 vendors), Intricate Carving Tool
  9295 (Misc, 10,000, burden 40; 14 vendors), Handle Mold 41396 (a crafting intermediate, 10,000, burden 100; 40 vendors), Tusker
  Island Shirt 22565 (Clothing, 9,000, burden 57; a vendor), Skull Cap 25522 (Armor, 8,500, burden 125), Virindi Scalpel 11007
  (MeleeWeapon, 8,420, burden 30), Healing Chocolate Olthoi Cake 11129 (Food, 8,000, burden 25), Entrancing Adjanite Gem 27771
  (Gem, 15,000, burden 25); none is rare, bonded or attuned (SELECT); total burden 452 of the mage's 19,650 capacity. A robe
  (Luminous Robe 31304, 13,000, burden 150) would also qualify as the Clothing decoy now that the scarab is not protected;
  the vendor's shirt was chosen because the owner asked for items people bought at a vendor. Eight decoys plus the Mana Scarab
  are nine items above the wand against at most eight drops, so one is always left over. The suicide's unbuff death (D33) drops
  items too: the kit tops the decoys up before the real death.
- *Order.* s0; `cap` (the lead's live capture); then s1, s2 and d0 as lanes; s3 and d1 as lanes (s3 needs s1 and s2, d1 needs d0);
  s4, p and d2 as lanes (s4 and p need s3, d2 needs d1); the lead's live round 1 beside s3 and d1; the review on the merged
  slices while the last lanes are written; the doc pass (Haiku, from the doc notes under `cache/m4/3-1/`) and the fix slice.
  Lanes run in their own worktrees on disjoint files (a lane edits only its row's files and its owner's set, §2); the shared
  seams are `OptionCatalog` (append only) and `tools/smoke.ps1` (s4 and d2 edit different steps).

### 4.8 Item 4.1 Auto-buff, the tuskers retired and the visual pass (branch `m4/4-1`; wave 4's first item, sliced by its lead at 8d44573)

The shape and rules of §4 and §4.7 hold. Precondition: 3.2 merged, the backend down, checkpoint 3 answered (D26). **The item
grew at the checkpoint (D26, D27):** besides DoD 14 it retires the stand-in tuskers (`m4-standin-tusker-retire`; the stand-in
vendors stay) and runs a visual pass on what the owner sees in the window: the bow drawn when it is wielded, no stray glow from the
Healer's Heart and its glow when it comes out, the stance that matches the wielded weapon (a wand or an orb is magic, never melee),
and the item art from the dats (the stand-in's items lose the placeholder icon 0x06000001 and model). **Research (the lead,
2026-10-22).** The Healer's Heart is an orb: weenie 8552 `orbhealer`, Setup 0x020009AD, a held Caster (ItemType 0x8000). A held
item's Setup light is never lit: `AvatarSet.SyncLight` requires `InWorld`, which is false for any object with a parent
(`AvatarBody.cs` 242; the likely half of "no glow when it comes out"); a held item is lost only when its holder's Setup lacks the
holding location (`AvatarBody.SetParent` 726, logged "has no holding location"), and the 3.2 logs hold no such line, so the bow's
cause is read live first (round 1, below). The toolbar's mode rule already gives Magic for a held caster
(`CombatModeRules.DefaultMode`, retail `GetDefaultCombatMode` 0x0056C050); the stand-in's melee stance is always HandCombat
(`StandInWorld.Containers.cs` 336-342). The appraisal carries no remaining time (ACE `AppraiseInfo.cs` 494-503: the item's
enchantment ids only), so the armour memory needs a ledger, not only an appraisal. The lead's read-only SELECT of the world
database (MariaDB alone, then stopped): `cache/m4/4-1/weenie-icons.tsv`, every weenie's Setup, Icon, IconUnderlay and the two
overlays (43,917 rows). **Order:** batch 1 as lanes: a, b, t1, v0, v4 (three Godot lanes at most: t1, v0, v4); the lead's live
round 1 (the look capture by v0's option on Holtburg Hunter and BSD Mage, and the driver unit-proved); batch 2 as lanes: c, t2, v1,
v3; the review from batch 1's merge; the fix slice and the Haiku doc pass side by side; the gate (round 2); round 3 spare.

### 4.10 The polish pass (branch `m4/p`; after 4.1, before 4.2: D29)

The items are docs/milestones/m4-polish-pass.md §1 (P1 the near-duplicate tests, P2 the bow live, P3 the look camera). **Order:** the surveys of P1 (read-only Sonnet, one per test area, no worktree) and the lead's live missile hunt for P2 at once; then the fold lanes of P1 (one per area, in their own worktrees cut from `m4/p`) and a P2/P3 slice if the live run finds a cause; a short Opus review; the gate (G-A, then the loopback suite and the full smoke once: the count drops, coverage does not, D28); the merge "Merge m4/p (Polish pass)". Each fold lane lists every row it deletes with the row that still proves its behaviour; nothing is folded that the survey did not list.

## 5. Gates and the live-gate rules

M3's §5 in full (m3-plan.md §5, including the "Changed in M3 4.2" paragraph: `-Backend require` implies `-SoloFrames`
and `-FullScan`; G-A is `slice-check -Smoke -MinTests <baseline>` and implies `-Exhaustive`; the frame judge is over
75 ms fails, over 50 ms is flagged, two of three runs over 50 ms fail the gate), with these M4 changes:

1. **Branches and worktrees:** `m4/<item>`, `.claude/worktrees/m4-<item>-<slice>`; the merged item's worktrees and
   branches are removed after its merge (M3 D31 carried, D15).
2. **The checkpoint after every wave (D3):** the wave's last lead writes the spend per item against §3, the DoD items
   met so far, the frames, and pings the owner; nothing is cut or started until the owner answers. The cut order the
   owner may apply: 4.1 auto-buff, then the examine panel (the model built, the panel `NotInM4`), then the drag (D4).
3. **New verdict fields and their first gates:** `swings shots hits misses returned fighter` report-only in 1.2, judged
   as DoD 3 and 4 say from 1.3; `corpses_opened` judged in 2.1's gate, `looted` from 2.2's second gate (3.1),
   `corpse_looted` report-only in 2.2; `heals kit_uses potions` from 3.1 (`heals>=1` judged at 3.2's gate);
   `appraised sold bought pyreals` report-only in 3.2, `sold>=1` judged at 4.2; `attack_frame_max_ms` report-only in
   1.3 and judged from 2.1, `loot_frame_max_ms` report-only in 2.2 and judged from 3.1, `vendor_frame_max_ms`
   report-only in 3.2 and judged at 4.2; each against the §5 rule (75 fails, 50 flags) on the worst frame of its window.
4. **The live gate's steps:** the loopback suite (`Category=Loopback&Tier!=Soak`; the first test asserts access Player
   and auto-promotion off), the frame judge (three runs of `smoke.ps1 -Only login-check,login -Backend require`), the
   full smoke once (`smoke.ps1 -Backend require -SoloFrames -FullScan -Parallel 2`; `login-hunt` joins `$backendSteps`
   in 1.2), the Soak tier once in 1.1 (the M2 soak run) and at 4.2; `stop-server.ps1` in a finally; the OpenAC gate
   checks of the wave's rows (openac-m4-map.md §2) read in the gate's log.
5. **Mod installs (D8):** when an item changes `server/mod`, the lead runs `setup-server.ps1 -ModOnly -ModSource <the
   item worktree>\server\mod` after the mod tests pass, and a plain `setup-server.ps1 -ModOnly` after the merge; each
   install is a line in §8; never `-AllowStaleMod`. 1.1 is the one planned install (the fighter's name).
6. **Captures before trackers:** each wave's wire slice is briefed after the lead's loopback capture of the real order
   on this server (spec §5's rule); a disagreement with the spec is written into the brief and the spec's §5 row.
7. **Rounds, not marathons:** an agent whose round would pass 300K of context ends with its handoff and a fresh agent
   continues (M3 §8 item 4.2: p5a, p6 and h ran over; fix B ran four rounds). At most three live rounds per item, then
   the lead stops, writes the numbers into §8 and asks the owner.
8. **The owner's eye on every frame:** the lead sends every review frame at 800x600 and checks, before sending, the
   held items and the models (the player holding a copy of itself happened twice in M3: 4.1 fix A, 4.2 p6), saying
   plainly what is a placeholder.

**Changed in P4 (D31: the parallel live gate, `tools\live-gate.ps1`).** Its lanes are superseded by "Changed in P5b" below. The gate runs in two phases. The PARALLEL phase
runs the three test accounts at once, one lane each (`test`, `test-melee`, `test-archer`), in order inside the lane: the
account's loopback classes, then its smoke steps (`test`: login-throwaway, login-buff, login-bsd, login-death;
`test-archer`: login-hunt-missile with `--hunt-kills=2`). Its frame times are reported, not judged (`-ContendedFrames`).
The SOLO phase, one step at a time with nothing else running, judges: the frame judge three times
(`login-check,login`), one melee hunt (`login-hunt` on the Brawler, with its review frames and frame fields judged), the
movement-precision loopback classes (`Lane=solo`), the throwaway's teleport frame three times (judged by the three-run
rule: two of three runs over 50 ms fail; over 75 ms fails), and the leak scan (`stray-output -FullScan`), which reads the
whole gate folder and the dotnet test logs, not only its own. Each lane's smoke calls run with `-NoScan` so that three scans
never read files another lane is writing. No step repeats: G-A's offline smoke steps are not re-run; the loopback suite runs
by area (`-Loopback core` at most items, `full` at a wave's last item and the milestone end); the hunt runs once per weapon,
each with its own review frames; `login-throwaway` runs in the solo phase only. `-Serial` keeps the old order (the loopback
suite with `LIFESTONE_LOOPBACK_SESSION=per-test`, the frame judge three times, then `smoke.ps1 -Backend require -SoloFrames
-FullScan -Parallel 2`) for a comparison on the same commit. The `-Soak` tier runs once, alone, last (4.2).

The new judges are judged in their first gate as named exceptions to "a new judge is report-only in its first gate",
decided by the lead's direction (D31): (a) the audio check, a fact and not a threshold: every
automated Godot run must log `audio service installed ... muted=true`, and a run that does not fails its step; (b) the
missile hunt's existing judged fields on its new character (`ok`, `kills>=2`, `looted>=1`, `shots>=1` with `hits>=1`),
which were proved live twice on the Archer before the gate. The other new fields stay report-only in their first gate:
`blocked=`, `seeks=`, `frames=contended` (a PASS in the parallel phase) and the parallel phase's frame fields. The
frame rule is unchanged (over 75 ms fails, over 50 ms is flagged).

**Changed in P5b (the journeys gate).** The live gate's PARALLEL phase has four lanes, one per account, with at most three Godot windows at once (muted, one `-UserDataSlot` each): `test` runs its short loopback classes (the raw-net, headless, stats, inventory, item-move and mod-verb classes, the Academy quest facts and the Hunter's build) and then `smoke -Only journey-mage -ContendedFrames`; `test-melee` runs `journey-melee` alone; `test-archer` runs Shortcut's relog class and then `journey-archer`; `test-tools` (headless) runs the throwaway's and the mod-verb classes. The SOLO phase judges: the frame judge (`login-check,login`, three runs, the 75 ms and 50 ms rule unchanged), LivePhaseB's two raw-net walks (`Lane=solo`), the throwaway's frames (three runs, on `test-tools`) and the leak scan. `-JourneySolo <mage|melee|archer>` moves one journey from its lane into SOLO, so its station frames are judged rather than contended. Gone from the gate: the solo melee hunt, `login-bsd`, `login-buff`, `login-death`, `login-hunt-missile` and the solo movement loopback (the laps and the exit are judged solo inside `login-check=all`). J6 deleted the retired area's classes; their coverage became the journeys' stations, and the three single facts the old area skipped by name (Container's chest, Shortcut's in-session fact, AcademyExit's exit walk) are deleted. `-Serial` runs the three journeys headless, one after another (`dotnet run --project src/Lifestone.Headless -- --scenario=journey --journey=<name>`), judged by `smoke.ps1`'s `Get-JourneyVerdict` with the same done-line rule as the Godot step, with the frame judge three runs and the leak scan. `-CompareTo <tag>` prints each journey's headless and Godot done lines side by side in the gate log. The projection (`-Plan`): ExpectedSeconds `loopback:test` 150, `journey-mage` 1,100, `journey-melee` 900, `journey-archer` 1,000, `loopback:test-archer` 70 and `loopback:test-tools` 500; the parallel phase about 1,250 s (the mage's lane bounds it), the solo phase about 810 s, about 2,060 s in all; every limit is twice its step. The new judges are report-only in their first gate: each journey row ends `[report-only route_m= rungs= teleports= recalls= granted= recovery_places= vitals_passes= run_s= keeper...]`, and the judged fields stay the journey step's own (done ok, `failed=none`, teleports within the budget, no failed station). The frame rule (over 75 ms fails, over 50 ms flags, two of three runs flagged fail) and the live-gate rules above stand.

**Changed in the fresh-gate item (D44-D46).** **G-A before every live gate round is `slice-check -Gate`**: the build, the default tests and the Exhaustive tier, no Godot step, ending with one `offline SKIP` line that names the steps the diff selects (measured 2 min 23 s to 4 min 11 s; the old G-A, `slice-check -Smoke`, took about 15 min). **The offline Godot smoke runs once per item**, after its last live gate and before the merge: `slice-check -Changed` (the steps `tools\lib\StepPaths.psd1` selects for the item's diff), or `slice-check -Smoke` at a wave's last item and the milestone end. **Each journey lane starts with `recreate`** (D44): `test` runs recreate (`--scenario=mage-build --fresh-character`), its loopback classes, `journey-mage`; `test-melee` recreate (`--scenario=fighter-build --account=test-melee --fresh-character`), `journey-melee`; `test-archer` recreate, Shortcut's relog class, `journey-archer`; `test-tools` is unchanged. `-Serial` runs `recreate-<name>` before each headless journey; `-JourneySolo` leaves the recreate at the head of its lane. A recreate passes on exit 0, `headless done scenario=<s> ok=1` and a verdict line with `fresh=recreated|created` and `purged=<n>` (`GateJudge.psm1` `Get-RecreateVerdict`); a journey whose recreate failed is not run (one FAIL row, `not run: the re-create on <account> failed`); `-KeepCharacters` plans no recreate and says so once. ExpectedSeconds `recreate:fighter` 90 and `recreate:mage` 120 (limits twice); the projection (`-Plan`) is parallel 1,170 s, solo 810 s, 1,980 s in all. Measured in round 2: the recreates 27 s (mage) and 61-62 s (fighters); the gate 1,613 s (setup 18, parallel 1,059, solo 534). The fresh characters end one level above their templates (mage 127, fighters 56: the build's last step is a level-up, so ACE refills every vital), and each journey makes its one budgeted teleport again (the mage's den hop and tie). The frame rule and the live-gate rules above stand.

The lead's proof timings (the whole loopback suite: 2,610 s per-test and 2,573 s shared, at ebee55d) are the baseline for the
first parallel run's wall time.

## 6. The rules and the process

The rules are CLAUDE.md, "Hard rules" (fidelity under "Quality") and "Rules for agent sessions"; a brief cites them
and restates nothing. The templates in `cache/m4-prompt-templates.md` (gitignored; M3's T1-T7 with the names changed)
carry a rules block whose lines must equal CLAUDE.md's; a lead compares the two at the start of its item and corrects
the template, so the canonical text is CLAUDE.md's.

The process decisions are M3's P1-P8 (m3-plan.md §6) with D38's model mix (Haiku 5.5 for merges without code
conflicts, doc passes, read-only lookups and cleanup; Sonnet 5.5 for code slices, lanes, fixes and cluster reviews;
Opus 5.5 at high effort, set explicitly, for the per-item lead and the item review) and these M4 amendments: the
checkpoint after every wave (D3, amending P6's "at each wave boundary" to a ping with at most four questions and the
spend); a planned research phase per item (the lead's captures and SELECTs, cluster-style read-only lanes when an item
audits, as 4.2 did); the roadmap line at every transition; frames judged solo (M3 D42). Templates: **T1** the wave
lead prompt (one fresh session per item, with its settings table); **T2** the implementer slice brief; **T3** the
parallel lane brief; **T4** the review brief; **T5** the fix-slice brief; **T6** the merge brief; **T7** the live-gate
runbook. Each lead writes the next item's T1 into `cache/` before it ends. Should `cache/` be lost, the templates are
rebuilt from m3-plan.md §6, §4's shape and §5.

overrule at any checkpoint. The M3 decisions M4 relies on are m3-plan.md §7 (D3, D5, D8, D13, D19, D26, D34, D35,
D37, D38, D41, D42).

## 8. As run

**Planning (2026-10-19).** One Fable 5.1 session at high effort in plan mode, read-only until the docs were written;
one Explore agent (Haiku-class, 21 calls, 77K) checked the spec's "Lifestone today" column against master 52e5fb8 (it
holds: of the M4 messages only 019A and 00A0 are decoded and 01BF sent); the OpenAC watch (foundation-openac.md §6.4)
by `gh api` found the head moved to f22b048 (signed) with no ported file changed and the #218 revert (D7, D16); the
owner answered the batch of eight in two screens. The spend is the owner's meter reading at the session's end.

(Each lead appends its item's line: what changed from the table, the agent size table, the gate numbers, the live
rounds, the mod installs, the spend by the meter.)

**Item 1.1, the loop's remainder and the fighter (2026-10-19/20; merged as "Merge m4/1-1 (The loop's remainder and
the fighter)").** One Opus 5.5 lead at high effort.

- *Research (the lead, read-only: MariaDB alone by `start-server.ps1 -DatabaseOnly`, then stopped; the dats through a
  scratch probe outside the repository; `cache/m4/1-1/research.md`).* The kit: Mace 331 (Heavy Weapons 44, one-handed,
  burden 675, Value 260; Renald 718 buys MeleeWeapon), Shortbow 307 (Missile Weapons 47, 450, 200), Arrow 300 (5, 1;
  stack 1000), the leather set 45, 47, 81, 56, 115 (burden 2,560), Handy Healing Kit 628 (50, 10; 20 uses), Potion of
  Healing 377 (15, 170). The 11.6096 SkillTable offers all four creation skills (ChargenUse): Aluvian Heavy Weapons
  specialised 12, Missile Weapons 6, Healing 6, Melee Defense 10, so 34 of 52 credits (D13 holds as expected). **The
  belt, corrected:** ACE rolls a generator's children by cumulative probability (`WorldObject_Generators.cs:120-213`),
  so the Low B belt's band is 8-20, not the spec's 15-20, and the Tumerok Warrior's share is 0.06; generator 1999 is a
  ring 4.5-8 map units around Holtburg (709 cells within 8 units) and the spec's §5.9 box holds 6 cells; the ten
  south-west cells nearest town are in the 1.2 lead prompt and the research note.
- *What changed from the table.* s1 and s3 ran as lanes beside s0 (disjoint files; an hour saved), then s2 and s4 as
  lanes; `FighterTemplateDatTests` moved to `tests/Lifestone.Client.Tests/Session/Fighter/` (the template is the
  Client's); s2 proved the build offline on the scripted ACE (`ScriptedFighter`), not on `StandInWorld` (no login or
  enter-world in the stand-in, as for the mage; review R7); the lead wired `--scenario=fighter-build` in
  `src/Lifestone.Headless/Program.cs` (outside s2's files); s4's edit landed in `Client/RetailUi` (`GamePlayScreen`,
  `UiStagedRoot`, a new `NoGcWindow`) and s1's in `ModSourceTests.cs` (a pinned list literal): both outside their
  slices' globs, both benign (review R12), and 1.3's lead should know `GamePlayScreen` holds the window. The s4 probe
  ran from the item worktree against the main checkout's backend (the main checkout is master until the merge).
- *Agents* (calls / peak context): lookup Haiku Explore 27 / 110K; s0 22 / 151K; s1 15 / 86K; s3 67 / 208K (a hung
  `tools\commit.ps1 -h` left in its shell, stopped by the lead); merge s1+s3 Haiku 11 / 66K; s2 83 / **289K (ran
  long**: the scripted ACE model and five offline scenario tests in one round, just under the line); s4 21 / 101K;
  merge s2+s4 Haiku 12 / 66K; s5 37 / 119K; doc pass Haiku 20 / 99K; review Opus 5.5 (high) 47 / 211K; fix 24 / 120K.
- *Review.* 0 blockers, 6 should-fix, 8 nits. Fixed: R1 (the window's budget sized from the server-GC heap count,
  floor 240 MiB, cap 512 MiB, `UNDERSIZED` named as an estimate), R2 (the first collection after the window measured
  on the game thread's frames), R3 (an owner for the region, the window ended on a throw, off under the test host,
  `NoGcWindowTests`), R4 (the test says its xp and advancement assertions are live only on a build run). The evidence

**Item 2.2, loot in the loops and the inventory (2026-10-21; merged as "Merge m4/2-2 (Loot in the loops and the inventory)").**
One Opus 5.5 lead at high effort; wave 2's last lead (it ran checkpoint 2).

- *Research (the lead).* ACE's rot times and the shard's corpse settings by source and read-only SELECT
  (`cache/m4/2-2/research.md`): a player corpse with items rots after `max(3600, level x 300)` s, an empty one after 15 s
  (`W/Corpse.cs:103-118`), a monster corpse after the default 300 s (`W/WorldObject_Decay.cs:18`); `corpse_destroy_pyreals`
  is on (a death corpse holds no pyreals). Renald the Elder 718's MerchandiseItemTypes is 0x480467A7 (value 0 to 1,000,000).
  **The capture** (`LootCaptureLiveTests`, Tier=Capture, slice `cap`, one run of 2 m 21 s; `cache/m4/2-2/capture.md`)
  corrected the plan twice: four pick-ups from one corpse in a row are all accepted when the lane holds each next move
  1.46-1.48 s after the previous 0022 (the 1.35 s pickup motion plus the margin; no busy refusal), and **ACE never merges a
  picked-up stack at pick-up** (arrows and coins: a separate stack with a normal 0022), so `m4-pickup-merge` closed by
  removing the speculation. The first live round (the lead, beside slice s3) read the death trip and the hunt's loot
  (`cache/m4/2-2/live1-findings.md`): the mage's corpse is re-created for the client 1.0 s after the arrival, six items, four
  taken in about 21 s before the 40% health abort among the tuskers; the unprotected arrival (ACE dispels the lifestone
  protection on every teleport, `Player_Location.cs` 702-703) costs about half the health in 25 s.
- *What changed from the table.* A `cap` slice ran first (BSD Mage's corpse was recorded by the first live round, not a
  capture test). The order became `cap`, then s0 and s2 as lanes, then s1 and s3 as lanes, then s4 (the table had s0, s1 and
  s2 together): s1 reuses s0's shared `CorpseLooter` (new; the table had the loop inside the hunt) instead of a second loop.
  s0 also retired the merge branch of `PickUpTracker`. s2 found that `gm3DItemsUI` is the inventory page's own list: the
  container window is `gmExternalContainerUI` (0x1000000D) in `gmFloatyEnvPanelUI` (0x1000004E), no new art; the item list's
  examine stays `NotInM4` (Identify and the examine panel are 3.2's, D6). s3's drag covers the pack, side packs, container
  window, paperdoll slots and the world; #219 is not applicable (the toolbar holds no item shortcuts: pinned by a test).
  The Opus review ran beside the last slice on the merged slices and read s4 and the first fix by message; the lead's first

  the arrival is not ACE's 75% restore, which comes 3 s later); two fix slices followed (the second for the floaty stack).

  s1 129 / 424K; s3 138 / 437K; s4 201 / 445K; review Opus 5.5 (high) 44 + 13 / 305K + 375K; doc pass Haiku 38 / 247K;
  fix 218 / 481K (**the longest**; eight findings, three of them with new state machines); fix2 95 / 202K. Ten agents; none
  was cut mid-edit. Lessons: keep a code slice to four or five items with their tests; the Write and Edit tools can write
  CRLF (two source-text tests failed once; the merges were checked with `git ls-files --eol`); never run a second Godot beside
  G-A (one play-checks frame failed under the review agent's `dotnet test`: 13.6 ms; the solo rerun passed at 6.3 ms); a

**Item 3.1, recovery and the hunt loop (2026-10-21; merged as "Merge m4/3-1 (Recovery and the hunt loop)").** One lead at high effort (the session ran as Sonnet 5.5, not the Opus the settings table names); wave 3's first item.

- *Research (the lead).* Kit and potion facts by SELECT (Handy Healing Kit 628: 20 uses, boost 50, consumed at 0; Potion of Healing 377: boost 25; ACE's `Healer.cs`), the forest's generator tree (21183: 40 creatures, 6,618 spots world-wide, the 0x9FB* strip 7.7-9.2 map units west), ACE's death-drop rule from `DeathItem.cs` and `Player_Death.cs`, a spell-table lookup (the Mana Scarab is used only by level VIII spells no loop casts; the Greater Amuli Shadow pieces and the Platinum Scarab are bonded), ACE's skill-use experience rule (`Proficiency.cs`: round(1.1 x the other party's skill)). **The capture** (`HealCaptureLiveTests`, 3 m 27 s) corrected the plan: a heal is busy until the Ready F74C (UseDone at +1.48 s, a repeat at the UseDone is refused 0x001D); a failed roll still spends a use and 5 of 6 rolls failed under about 45% health (the kit needs the health high); a potion restores exactly its 25 and is spent even at a full vital; BSD Mage's kit use ends 0x04FC; the forest's level-30 creatures dealt about 150 of the hunter's 188 health in 3 hits while it dealt about 4 a hit, so **the forest is the damage source for the heal tests and the Low B belt stays the kill ground**.
- *What changed from the table.* Order: s0, cap, then s1, s2, d0 as lanes, then s3 and d1, g (the combat-mode guard, **added at the owner's request**, D23), then s4, p, d2 as lanes, the review, two fix slices and two doc passes. d1 composed `FightScenario` behind an injectable factory and took two follow-ups (the pre-trip Heal Self cast and the decoy top-up after the suicide: the new order ends buffed, so every next run suicides first and the suicide drops decoys too). The owner's mid-item answers: the reading of D20 confirmed, the decoy items kept (his "decoys" were the stand-in's monsters: D23), the ask-line raised to 6.5% then 7%, the hotbar and the empty boxes recorded for the next pass (D24). The recovery rule heals early and chooses kit or potion by ACE's success chance (the kit while the chance is at least 0.7, a potion otherwise, the kit as a last resort from 0.3 when no potion is left: review R1); the in-fight heal is not built (`TODO(m4-hunt-infight-heal)`).
- *Agents* (calls / tokens, as the harness reported): xp lookup Haiku 33 / 145K; s0 89 / 244K; scarab lookup 48 / 173K; cap 58 / 274K; d0 87 / 314K; s1 84 / 315K; s2 93 / 353K; **d1 175 / 546K (the longest: the composition into the 1,668-line `DeathScenario` and two follow-ups)**; s3 97 / 378K; **g 154 / 384K (the guard broke 66 unit rows and 14 play-check rows, fixed in the rows)**; **p 153 / 380K (the projection cause)**; s4 118 / 366K; **d2 184 / 371K (and ten repeated hand-back notices until stopped)**; review Opus (high) 56 + 28 / 314K + 427K; doc pass Haiku 47 / 366K and 50 / 202K; fix 160 / 391K; fix2 74 / 257K. Eighteen agents; none cut mid-edit. Lessons: a combined tree breaks what lanes proved alone (g's guard broke three d1 tests only the merge showed: test every merge); never build in the item worktree while a live test uses its binaries (a Windows file lock fails the build); a finished agent can keep re-sending its report (TaskStop it); the hunter's loot adds duplicates of kit weenie classes, which a first-match lookup cannot survive (fix2); a live test that depends on a random spawn must say INCONCLUSIVE, not pass.
- *Review.* 25 findings, 0 blockers, 8 should-fix (R1 the kit with no potion, R2 gear left on the corpse failed a new judge, R3 the decoy check's TODO names unlogged, R4/R5 pending lanes, R17 a duplicated smoke header line, R18 a heal test that passed green when skipped, R19 the headless limit), the rest nits. All fixed by the fix slice or the lead except the doc-only nits (R6, R11, R13, R14, R22, R23 are in §9 and the docs).
- *Gates.* G-A (`slice-check -Smoke`, solo): build 0 warnings, Exhaustive 76, 9,074 tests (floor 9,052), smoke ALL PASS 13 min, secrets 0 (205 files). Live: **round 1** (beside the lanes) `HuntLiveTests` and `DeathLiveTests` passed (the new loop live: `rebuffed=yes den_kills=4 decoys=8 decoy_safe=yes decoys_dropped=6 gear_dropped=0 decoys_recovered=6 corpse_looted=yes items_recovered=7 trip_abort=none`); **round 2** `HealLiveTests` and the 3-kill belt loop 4 of 4 (the kit failed 2 rolls at 14% health and a potion restored 25; the mage refused 0x04FC; `kills=3 looted=8 loot_skipped=3`); **round 3, the gate**: loopback 34 passed, 7 failed, 1 skipped (30 min), all seven the hunter's duplicate-class defect, the full smoke 26.8 min with `login-hunt` and `login-throwaway` (58.1 ms) failing, the frame judge three runs all PASS (login-check enter-to-walk 26.7 / 36.4 / 37.1 ms, login 39.5 / 45.4 / 37.9), the suicide path live in the smoke's `login-death` (`suicides=1 decoy_top_up_grants=6`, 427.9 s); **round 4** (the owner's go-ahead after fix2): loopback 40 passed, 1 INCONCLUSIVE (`HealLiveTests`: no in-band forest creature near its 4 cells), smoke `login-hunt` PASS (`kills=3 looted=8 heals=1 kit_uses=1 loot_frame_max_ms=4.2 attack_frame_max_ms=7.6 mode_mismatch=0`), `login-throwaway` 66.3 ms; then the A/B: `HealLiveTests` PASS (healed 1, 1 failed roll, a potion 25: 28.7% to 80.9%) and the throwaway step on the branch PASS 40.0 / FAIL 60.1 ms against master FAIL 66.7 / PASS 10.3 ms: **the teleport frame is the machine's noise, not this item** (`login-burst-frame` stays a guard). Judged: `looted>=1` over three kills, `loot_frame_max_ms`, `attack_frame_max_ms`, `corpses_opened`; report-only: `heals kit_uses potions`, the death leg's new fields, `mode_mismatch`.
- *Defect found by the gate and its fix (fix2).* `HuntScenario.FindItem` returned the first item of a weenie class and the held list puts the pack before the equipped items, so with a looted second Shortbow the swap waited for ever, ACE refused the Mace while a launcher was wielded, and every later hunter test failed (the combat-mode guard refusing melee with a Shortbow wielded, correctly). `FighterItems.Find` now prefers the equipped instance, the swap's restore repairs a half-done swap and the fighter build takes a stray launcher off first (it repaired the live hunter).
- *The OpenAC checks of this item's rows* (openac-m4-map.md §2): #214 (a logout or login near buildings), #139 and #154 (the recall arrival and moving off the lifestone): read in the live logs, no defect seen (`corrections=0 where_max_m=0.03` in login-check, the death loop's respawns, trips and returns ran five times live without a stuck character).
- *Frames* (800x600, checked for held items and models): the BSD Mage's inventory page with the decoys listed (`decoys`), its corpse window "Contents of Corpse of BSD Mage" with the seven recovered items (`corpse-open`), the death hit, dead and lifestone frames, and the hunter's main-pack panel shot listing its looted items (the Shortbow shows in the weapon slot: the stuck state before fix2). No copy of the player, no stray model. **Not taken live:** the hunt's combat, power-bar, meter, heal and potion frames and the hunt `corpse` frame: the projection now runs (`target_projected=True`, `m4-hunt-frame-camera` narrowed) but the camera rule finds the target hidden (0.1 m from the player) and the swing frame lands on a tree; the hunter was not hurt at the frame run. The empty cells of the corpse window and the paperdoll's slots draw as plain areas and outlines: D24's evidence.
- *Spend.* The meter read 25% of the week at the start and 31% at the merge: about 6% against the 5% estimate (the death-loop change, the polish slice, the owner's guard, the fourth round); the ceiling the owner approved was 6.5% and then 7%.

**Item 3.2, vendors and appraisal (2026-10-21/22; merged as "Merge m4/3-2 (Vendors and appraisal)").** One Opus 5.5 lead at high effort; wave 3's second item, with the owner's D24 (the hotbar and the empty boxes) inside it by D25.

- *Research (the lead).* Read-only SELECTs with MariaDB alone (`cache/m4/3-2/vendors.tsv`): Thelnoth's and Renald's cells, positions, use radii (3 m, 5 m), rates (0.9 / 1.35), masks (0x44080, 0x480467A7 = `LootRules`' value) and stock (every row unlimited; Thelnoth's 31 service rows); Holtburg Hunter's 10,000 pyreals and about 80 items of old loot; no stored shortcut bar. ACE's shortcut actions (`Player_Character.cs` 245-270: ACE answers nothing and never removes a row itself); the toolbar's, the vendor window's and the examination window's retail functions all have 11.6096 addresses in the symbol table. **The capture** (`VendorCaptureLiveTests`, 3 m 9 s; `cache/m4/3-2/capture.md`, now in the spec's §5.6 and §5.8): the UseDone precedes the 0062 by 0.65-1.04 s; a buy's coins and 0022 come before its UseDone and the item's F745 about 3 ms after; a sale's refreshed 0062 comes before the coins at Thelnoth and after the UseDone at Renald; the payout is a new pyreal stack; an unsellable item is 02EB, then 00A0 naming the player, then UseDone 0; every Identify answers in 1-10 ms with no busy hold, a repeat at once, a missing guid flags 0 success 0, guid 0 nothing; the shortcut list persists to the next login.
- *What changed from the table.* D24 added three rows: **e** (the empty boxes: the container row and the spell bars are growing lists that retail pads with `UiItemList::UpdateEmptySlots` 0x004E4390; Lifestone deleted the surplus cells), **h1** (the shortcut model and `AddShortcut`/`RemoveShortcut`/`UseShortcut`, #219 in force) and **h2** (the toolbar's bar of nine: digits 1-9, Alt+1-9 for slots 10-18, the drag, the template's own number and cooldown art). Order: s0 and e at once; cap and h1; s1, s2, s3; s4a, s4b and h2 (three Godot lanes at most), then s4c; the review from the first merged batch, in three messages; the fix slice and the Haiku doc pass side by side; a second doc pass; fix2 at the gate. s4a sells after `wind-restore` (the Mace wielded again) and buys after the sale. D23: no stand-in creature; the stand-in's two vendors stand at the real ones' places and are tagged "(stand-in placeholder)" on screen.
- *Agents* (calls / tokens, as the harness reported): s0 95 / 329K; e 107 / 277K; cap 35 / 224K; h1 128 / 338K; s1 106 / 345K; s2 73 / 247K; s3 85 / 348K; s4a 118 / 392K; **s4b 188 / 463K (the vendor window's three pages from the layout, the sell list's drop rule)**; **s4c 189 / 469K (the examination floaty and the paperdoll's click map from the dats)**; **h2 223 / 513K (the toolbar's lists and the keymap from the dats, a new drag seam)**; review Opus (high) 68 + 27 + 13 + 13 / 394K at the last round; doc pass Haiku 102 / 327K and 22 / 145K; fix 140 / 322K; fix2 30 / 98K. Sixteen agents; none cut mid-edit. The three panel lanes ran long because each read a retail window's layout from the dats before building it; a panel lane is two slices' work. Lessons: every merge of the batch-1 lanes conflicted in the shared append points (`ActionApi`, `IActionApi`, `ActionKind`, `ActionPolicy`, the fakes, the kinds list, the events catalogue), and twice the merge shared a method's closing lines between the two sides (a union needs the shared tail repeated); two lanes named a test helper `VendorRig` in one namespace (only the combined build showed it); a lane's play-checks frame-time failures while other lanes ran Godot were contention (solo 210/210); a live test that relies on what the hunter carries breaks when the hunt's own sale empties the pack (fix2: the test grants its own items).

- *Agents* (calls / tokens, as the harness reported): survey (Explore, Sonnet) 47 / 144K; a 65 / 217K; b 65 / 215K; t1 about 150 / 305K (two rounds: the session exit); c about 120 / 287K (two rounds: an expired sign-in, 401); v4 about 180 / 338K (three rounds: the exit, then the chest); v0 about 150 / 334K (two rounds); fix 109 / 313K; v3 112 / 309K; t2 133 / 373K; v1 133 / 309K; doc pass Haiku 57 / 214K and about 60 / 200K; **fix2a 111 / 420K (over the line: the shop rows went to fix2c)**; fix2b 93 / 245K; fix2c 53 / 252K; fix3 38 / 124K; review Opus (high) four rounds in one agent to 394K, then a fresh Opus reviewer for round 5 58 / 263K. Twenty-one agents; none cut mid-edit. Lessons: a session exit or an expired sign-in stops every agent, and each one resumes from its worktree by a message (no work was lost); a reviewer past about 350K hands over to a fresh one that reads the review file; a deletion of tests must name where each behaviour is proved, or its rebuild costs more than the deletion saved (D28).
- *Review.* Five rounds: 27 findings, 0 blockers; should-fix R1 (the placeholders), R2-R5 (the driver at login, the stale store, no armour, a stale TODO), R8 (the examine icon an addition to retail), R17 (the deleted hunt rows), R18 (docs), R22 (docs after fix2c); all fixed; nits fixed or in §9 (R19, R21 open).
- *Gates.* G-A (`slice-check -Smoke`, solo, 0154339): build 0 warnings, Exhaustive 76, 9,606 tests, smoke ALL PASS 10.9 min. **Live round 2, the gate** (0154339): loopback 44 passed, 1 failed, 1 skipped (`HealLiveTests`: no Potion of Healing left in the hunter's pack, the hunts had drunk them; a state precondition, not this item's code), `RebuffLiveTests` passed; the look capture: the Healer's Heart `light=on` in hand in peace and Magic, `light=off particles=0` stowed; the Mace SwordCombat, the Shortbow BowCombat with its arrow; the hunt frames `ok=true kills=3 looted=5 sold=5 mode_mismatch=0 attack_frame_max_ms=9.7 vendor_frame_max_ms=9.4` (`heals=0`: no potion and a kit chance under the rule's floor at 18% health); the frame judge three runs PASS (enter-to-walk at most 44.5 ms); the full smoke ALL PASS 28.3 min, with **`login-buff` `ok=true buffs=24/24 rebuffed=yes forced=no driver=on rebuff_lines=4 rebuff_s=34`** (DoD 14). The final offline check after fix3 (951cbd7): 9,609 tests, play-checks PASS on the rerun (the first run failed two frame-time rows, the teleport frame and enter-after-select, beside the check's own shell-frames step), shell-frames 10/10. **Live round 3**: `FighterBuildLoopbackTests` topped the hunter's kit stacks up (`fighter=built level=40`, not levelled), then `HealLiveTests` 2 of 2 passed. Judged: `rebuffed=yes` from the driver; report-only: `forced=no driver= rebuff_lines= rebuff_s=` (judged at 4.2), the look lines.
- *Frames* (800x600, checked for held items and models): live, the hunter's Shortbow drawn with its arrow, the mage holding the lit Healer's Heart, the hunter's swing at an Auroch Cow; offline, the pack, the chest and the vendor with the dat icons (the stand-in player and the vendors' human models are labelled placeholders). No copy of the player, no stray model, no creature in an offline frame.
- *Pushes* (the owner's asks, during the item): master's M4 waves 1-3 as ca95309, and the README brought up to date as 02d44f7 (by the publish method; 4.1 not included).
- *Spend.* The meter read 36% of the week at the start and 42% at the merge: about 6% against the 5% estimate (auto-buff 2%, the tuskers 0.5%, the visual pass 2.5%), at the ask-line of 6% (D27).

**The polish pass, P1-P3 (2026-10-22; merged as "Merge m4/p (Polish pass)").** Run by 4.1's lead in the same session (D29), before 4.2; the owner widened it twice (D30, D31).

- *What was built.* **P1 and P1b, the tests (D28, D30):** three read-only surveys measured the near-duplicates (about -680 predicted); three fold lanes deleted 623 (each deletion named with the row that still proves it); the owner judged that this was not a big drop, so four cut lanes followed: A the prose pins (6 tests and the wording asserts of 17), B one golden-line test per leg verdict (-11), C the stand-in's protocol tests no play-check relies on (-103), D 588 dat tests moved from the default run to the gate tier (nothing deleted). The review sampled 62 deletions and found 12 unproved (boundaries, loud stubs, capture values, review-id guards, a script pin); the restore lane swept every deletion by those patterns and put back 49. **Result: the default run 9,609 to 8,343 (-13%), the gate tier 76 to 664; the default stage about 60 s to 28 s.** The near-duplicate product code (`m4-degrade-frames-dup`) is a §9 row. **P2 and P3, the camera:** the bow was drawn but the play view was 60 degrees where retail's is 90/(aspect-0.1), 73 degrees at 800x600 (`SmartBox::RenderNormalMode` 0x00453B40, `SetDefaultFov` 0x00451EA0, `ComputeAspectForViewport` 0x0054FD60), so the player stood low behind the combat window; play now uses retail's view (`--fov` wins, explore keeps 60), the missile hunt's frames are framed (the bow drawn above the window), the look capture aims from the chest. Not fixed: the melee swing review frame came out empty at the gate (`m4-hunt-frame-camera`).
- *Agents* (calls / tokens): surveys 44 / 364K, 55 / 393K, 45 / 247K; folds 66 / 205K, 64 / 204K, 84 / 268K; cuts A 47 / 206K, B 34 / 163K, C 48 / 181K, D 39 / 118K; cam 117 / 289K; review Opus (high) 78 / 236K; fix restore 63 / 249K; fix docs 45 / 144K. Fourteen agents. Lesson: lanes that delete tests must be checked by sampling (12 of 62 unproved in the first pass), and a pattern sweep beats fixing the sample.
- *Gates.* G-A (`slice-check -Smoke`, b527b0d): build 0 warnings, Exhaustive 664, 8,294, smoke ALL PASS 10.8 min; after the fixes (635a5f1) 8,343 default and 664 Exhaustive green. **Live (round 2, the gate):** loopback 45 passed, 1 skipped; the look capture, the hunt frames (melee) and the missile frames (`ok=true kills=2 shots=6 hits=4 attack_frame_max_ms=10.8 player_above_window=True`), the panel shots, the frame judge three runs PASS; the full smoke 28.2 min with one failure, `login-throwaway`'s judged teleport frame (57.7 ms). **Round 3, the A/B** (solo, one commit): the teleport frame at 73 degrees 26.4 / 54.4 / 8.4 ms, at `--fov=60` 58.3 / 46.9 / 66.7 ms: it fails about half the time on either view, so it is the machine's noise (as 3.1's A/B said), not the view; P4's solo phase judges it over three runs.
- *Spend.* The meter read 42% at the pass's start and 44% at its merge: about 2% against the 2.5% estimate.

**Polish item P4, the parallel live gate (2026-10-22 to 23; branch `m4/p4`; merged as "Merge m4/p4 (The parallel live gate)" on the owner's word after four live rounds).** Run by its own lead from `cache/m4-p2-parallel-gate-lead-prompt.md` (D31).

- *What was built.* Two accounts (`test-melee`, `test-archer`; `setup-server -AddAccount`, secrets.json only; a read-only dump of the shard first) and two characters built live at level 40 by the template-driven fighter build: **Holtburg Brawler** (Heavy Weapons, the Mace, the Low B belt) and **Holtburg Archer** (Missile Weapons, the Longbow 31092 and Greater Frost Arrows 5307, the forest's levels 20-30, never the creatures that resist its arrows: ACE's ResistPierce and ResistCold); the hunt's seek (the owner's suggestion: it teleports next to the nearest band creature the client sees; the forest is ACE `encounter` spawns, about 3.5 points a landblock drawn from levels 8-80); per-shot outcomes, `blocked=`, `seeks=`; the long-lived headless session and one login per character in the loopback tests (`LIFESTONE_LOOPBACK_SESSION=per-test|shared`, the lock per account, the Account and Lane traits); `tools/live-gate.ps1` (PARALLEL by account, then SOLO; `-Serial`, `-Soak`, `-Loopback`, `-Plan`); smoke by account; the audio check; CLAUDE.md's Accounts rule (D31).
- *The proof of the shared session* (the whole loopback suite, sequential, one commit): round 1 per-test 2,499 s, shared 2,435 s (three shared-only bugs, fixed: the idle session dropped, an own-login test colliding, the cast's mana); round 2 per-test 2,610 s, shared 2,573 s; the three remaining tests then passed both ways. One login per character saves about a minute: the lanes are the gain.
- *Live rounds.* g1 (b01c45d): the lanes never ran (review R1, R2), 919 s. g2 (e7fc4a9): 16,955 s, two smoke steps hung on a child's inherited stdin until a 4 h timeout, the counts misread, frame failures misclassified, `login` failed by a shadowed variable, the Archer too weak (fixed: rfix-c, arch). **g3 (b4ea917): 3,304 s (parallel 1,964 s, solo 1,320 s) against about 4,860 s for the old order at 4.1;** the archer lane and its missile hunt (`kills=2 seeks=2`), the solo melee hunt (`kills=3`), the frame judge, login-bsd and the movement tests passed; three failed: DeathLiveTests (180 s with 3 of 4 kills, waiting for mana), HealBrawlerLiveTests (INCONCLUSIVE: one kit healed to full on the belt, so the potion was not drunk) and the throwaway's teleport frame (76.7 ms in one of three solo runs, over 75). The `-Serial` comparison on one commit is not run yet.

**Polish item P5a, wayfinding and the characters' abilities (2026-10-23 to 24; branch `m4/p5a`; six live rounds: the owner pre-authorised the fourth and chose the fifth and sixth).** Run by its own lead (D33-D42; the slice table is §4.11, the prose is `docs/wayfinding.md`, `docs/physics.md` section 16 and `docs/session.md` section 40).

- *What was built.* **The wayfinder:** `NavTile` and its builder (the ground, the statics, the buildings, the doorway lanes; the Holtburg tile 5.3 ms median), `NavMap` and `PathPlanner` (a corridor A*, scars, string pulling, the horizon; a 1 km plan 6.3 ms on dense ground), `RoomGraph` over the cell route planner (the den planner is gone), `NavRunner` (pursuit, the four-rung stuck ladder, arrival at a point or an object with sight) through `IActionApi.Move` and so the player's own keys (`MoveIntents.Bind` in both hosts), the corridor streamer for the headless host and `--scenario=wayfind` (Holtburg to the belt and back, to the forest and back). **The characters' abilities:** the two fighters train the three schools, Arcane Lore and Mana Conversion, learn the self-buffs and Lifestone Recall (`RecallHome`), carry a caster in the pack and swap it in (`CasterSwap`), are **re-created** with their own creation attributes (D40) at level 55, wear 13 mismatched retail magic pieces each (D41) and carry their spell components and mana and stamina potions in side packs; `VitalsKeeper` (health, stamina and mana, the rest posture, the fighters' mana order), `HarmSelfPlan` and `HarmHealScript` (harm to half, heal by kit, potion and spell; the fighters' and the mage's paths) and the fighters' buff leg (`--scenario=buff` on their accounts) complete it. The fighters now run at 9.6 to 10.5 m/s in the live wayfind runs instead of 4.8.
- *Changed from the table.* Slices H0, C1b, C1c and C4 were added; C5, C6 and C7 are the owner's additions (D40, D41); the review and six fix slices followed the live rounds (§4.11). The design's D39 (the fourth account) stays P5b's, as do the mage's travel (D36), the death station (D37) and the journeys.

The lesson, as in P4: a slice that gets owner additions mid-run outgrows its 300K.
- *Live rounds.* **r1** (7c41f26): the fighters rebuilt to level 55 (31 s each, second runs `fighter=ready`); the Brawler's wayfind out leg ARRIVED, 1,029.7 m in 107.2 s at 9.60 m/s, `stuck=0`; four failures, none in the planner or the runner: the end check's 0.6 m where tolerance (the query went out the instant the body stopped), the Archer's wayfind (two headless processes shared a log name), HealBrawler (login 0x0D, the script's order on one account) and HealArcher (`YouDontHaveAllTheComponents`: the fighters' kit had no components). **r2** (9dfcfe5): G-A build 0 warnings, 8,750 default, 707 Exhaustive, smoke ALL PASS 11 min; wayfind both fighters both ways, the Brawler 1,028 m (107.0 s and 108.7 s), the Archer 1,869 m (195.1 s and 193.5 s), about 9.6 m/s, `stuck=0` everywhere; **the gate 3,081 s** (parallel 1,952, solo 1,116), failing by class (`r2-diagnosis.md`): P5a's own, the Brawler's full pack (F-abil's 40 component stacks on an old full character: the hunt looted 0, and VendorLive, HuntLive, HealBrawler and the buff leg could not unwield) and the lane budgets (the archer lane killed at 600 s, the buff leg needing about 700 s), with the HealArcher harm cap; Revitalize Self cast for mana drained what each Stamina to Mana transfer gained: 16 transfers, 12 Revitalizes, mana 60 of 291); the Archer's buff leg took 12 min. **r6** (the owner's: short; 818cfd09): after C7, G-A 8,817 default, 715 Exhaustive, smoke ALL PASS 11.1 min; **Polish item P5b, journeys and the gate (2026-10-24; branch `m4/p5b`; three live gate rounds after an aborted round 0, the owner's word at each: one more round, gate B dropped, round 3, merge).** Run by its own lead (D36-D39, D43; the slice table is §4.12, the prose is `docs/session.md` section 41 and `docs/wayfinding.md`).

- *What was built.* **The journeys:** one login per character, stations on foot and by recall, one log off: the mage (build-verify, gear, buff, cast-facts, kit-refused, harm-heal, bind with Lifestone Tie, den-travel by Primary Portal Recall, den-fight, death (D37: undress, lie down, one death), recall-home), the melee (build-verify, buff, bind, the belt hunt, harm-heal, recall-home, vendor, vendor-facts, chests) and the archer (build-verify, buff, bind, the forest hunt, appraise-items, harm-heal, recall-home, appraise-vendor, vendor, shortcuts), and three hero plans for the video (`--journey-shots`). **The machinery:** the travel seam at the scenarios' teleport choke points (a place keeps its arrival check; a run, a recall or nothing is authoritative; one budgeted teleport refused loudly past the budget), the station kinds (`ScenarioStation` over the embedded scenarios, `ScriptLeg` stations ported from the loopback legs), `JourneyFacts` (the ties are not in the player description), the headless host and the Godot host over one `JourneyLegPlanner` (the settle gate, the indoor exit route, object goals confirmed to their range, the step-off from a raised start), the vitals keeper's casts inside the caster swap, the recall's busy wait. **The gate:** `test-tools` with Lifestone Throwaway and its purge caller Lifestone Tools (D39, D43), smoke's `journey-*` steps, `live-gate.ps1`'s four PARALLEL lanes and SOLO, the retired live classes deleted with the coverage table (D28).

- *Changed from the table.* See §4.12's closing sentence; G0 grew D43 (no purge caller on `test-tools`, the owner chose Lifestone Tools); the throwaway-frame fix came from a side session the owner started from the lead's proposal.

Five passed 300K, each with its reason: J2b (327K: the lie-down investigation), J3a (370K: six loopback files ported), J3b (345K: the builds plus the dat pins and the kit's headroom), J4 (327K: the WayfindScenario extraction and the catalog's 18 kinds), J7 (329K: the in-station frames lifted out of four leg runners plus the hero plans); FC-a reached 300K. The lesson, as in P5a: a slice that ports or lifts code from several old files outgrows its four items.
- *Live runs before the gate.* Round 1 of the lanes (the builds, the ported station facts, D37's one death `died=1 suicides=0 undressed=5 lie_down=sent lie_echo=yes death_s=42`, the `test-tools` lane, the throwaway on `test-tools`); journeys headless 1-3 and Godot (the first Godot journeys blocked on every walk: J5b), each fault to F1-F3; the hero clips (hero-mage: Cassius' Ring of Fire 3 kills, 54 projectiles, teleports=0; sent to the owner).
- *Gates.* **G-A** round 1 failed on ReplayTests (the vitae recording: F4); round 2 green (build 0 warnings, 8,992 default, 729 Exhaustive, smoke ALL PASS 11.2 min, secrets 0); rounds 3-4 the owner's shorter G-A (build, 9,002-9,011 unit tests, 732 Exhaustive; round 4 one pre-existing load flake, DynamicLights' allocation test, 3/3 alone). **The live gate** (the item worktree's `live-gate.ps1`, the backend from the main checkout): round 0 aborted (the lead ran master's script); **round 1** (44c6ee09) gate A 1,467 s, gate B (`-Serial`) 1,816 s: mage Godot buff and death, vendor-facts, the headless exits from Renald's shop, the archer's empty forest, the `test` lane's 300 s limit, the throwaway frame 2/3; **round 2** (c6ae094e, gate A only: the owner dropped gate B) 1,475 s: all but vendor-facts (the wrong Renald) and the throwaway frame (95.2 ms); **round 3** (717c3f32) **1,567 s (26.1 min)**: the mage 11/11 teleports=0 recalls=4, test-tools, every solo judge PASS (the throwaway frames 3 of 3 clean, login 3/3, login-check 2/3 with one 75.0 ms flagged, leak scan secrets 0); red: vendor-facts' cleanup (all four sub-checks held; fixed after the round, 2f9c84fb, proved by the next live journey) and the archer's hunt (no band creature within 35 m after 5 cells: the forest thinned by the day's hunts; `m4-hunt-band-empty`). The owner merged with both recorded.
- *The owner's decisions during the item.* D39 (from the design) and D43 (Lifestone Tools, §7); after gate round 1 (the meter at 66%): one more fix round and round 2; before round 2: gate B dropped, G-A cut to build and tests (its offline smoke stopped); after round 2: fix the vendor facts and run round 3, and bring the throwaway-frame fix in; after round 3: merge. Notes for later (cache/m4/p5b/decisions.md): the running looks unnatural and runs into enemies (a follow-up item); the automation must be outcome-aware (fizzle retry, Mana Conversion casts) and a patron bot (a design, cache/m5-patron-design.md); fresh characters each gate and a 3-minute G-A (a follow-up item). Five follow-up sessions were started by the owner from the lead's proposals.
- *Count.* 8,815 to 9,011 default (G1 and J6 deleted 9 default tests by name, the coverage table in `cache/m4/p5b/coverage.md`); Exhaustive 715 to 732.
- *Spend.* The weekly meter read 66% after gate round 1 and 69% at the merge (with five side sessions running); P5b's own share is estimated at about 9-10% against the 5.5% estimate (the owner approved going past the 6% ask-line after round 1).

**Polish follow-up, the wayfinder's feel (2026-10-24; branch `claude/pensive-rubin-af7c17`, cut from master 7d11705b after P5b's merge).** Run by one lead session the P5b lead started on the owner's note (`nav-natural-steering`, `nav-creature-avoid`); no slices. The prose is `docs/wayfinding.md` (the runner's straight run and turn key, the planner's straightening, the creature layer).

- *The owner's note* (2026-10-24, watching the journeys): the running looks unnatural, always turning where players ran in straight lines and turned rarely, and it seems unaware of the enemies and runs right into them.
- *The cause of the turning.* P5a held a turn key's release back as long as a press (0.2 s), and the body turns only when the physics steps (`MinQuantum`, 1/30 s: about 6.8 degrees a step at a running turn of 200 degrees a second), so a press overshot the 5 degree tolerance into the other side's key; with the 2 m lead every bend of the pulled route was a turn. Offline before (the streamer walks at 7.5 m/s): the belt 1,029.5 m with 468 turn presses and 415 key changes a minute, the forest 1,864.0 m with 806 and 394; the live journeys' bind walk 9 turns in 23 m, a hunt seek 20 in 76 m.
- *What changed.* **Steering:** outdoors the body aims at a route vertex (the farthest it may run straight to within max(10 m, 3 x speed) and one vertex past: skipped vertices within 2 m of the line, the lattice's sight at clearance 2 off water, slope and scars, no more creature cost than the route), a 6 degree dead-band, the next vertex within max(3 m, 0.4 s) of a corner; a held key is let go half a physics step early and a release is never held back; a straight run replanned off the route only past 6 m while it saw its aim (removed after the merge: the tracking below); doorways and rooms keep P5a's pursuit. **Planner:** the pulled route is straightened (a vertex goes when the kept one before sees the one after, at clearance 2). **Refusals** Block after three in a row, not three in the run: the 2 km forest run met two at its start under P5a too (small statics the tile under-stamps, `TODO(nav-static-footprint)`). **The creature layer:** `NavCreatures` (per run, never in the tile), `HostileCreatureFeed` (the radar's hostile rule; the run's goal creature left out), `CreatureAwareness` (ACE's `VisualAwarenessRange` for the 74 classes of the two hunt grounds by read-only SELECT, 8 to 35 m, else ACE's 18 m default, `Monster_Awareness.cs:377` @95ef426d; `Player.CheckMonsters` wakes a monster within it), a cost of 1 to 5 more a metre inside a disc, the pull never cutting deeper, the runner refreshing twice a second and replanning (at most every 2 s) when a creature the plan did not know meets the next 60 m. **Report-only fields:** `turns= key_changes_per_min= creatures_max= creature_replans= creature_min_m=` (the runner's verdict; turns and key changes on the wayfind leg line). **The clip hook:** `--journey-shots` bursts at `run` (a run on foot once it has gone 4 m, any station).
- *Offline after* (`HeadlessStreamerDatTests`, bounds asserted): the belt 1,019 m, 24 turns, 28 key changes a minute; the forest 1,846 m, 39 turns, 26 a minute; both arrived with 0 rungs. **After the merge** (354b0043; found by the fresh-characters item's G-A and the P5b lead's reruns): the belt walk stuck (`rungs=2`) in about one run in four: the body drifted 1.2 m off its confirmed route under the 6 degree dead-band and met static 0x02000258 in 0xA8B3, whose collision reaches past its stamp; the tile-attach timing on the streamer's workers decided which run drifted where. Fixed on `claude/nav-belt-stuck`: the straight run tracks back onto the route past 1 m (always, with the pursuit's short lead, where the lattice's clearance is 3 nodes or less), and the 6 m drift allowance is gone; twenty runs of the class, 0 rungs each, the belt 1,018.5 m with 53 turns and the forest 1,844.5 m with 76 (the turn bounds raised to 80 and 120, the stuck judge unchanged). `NavCreatureTests` (9): the planner goes round a creature it would have crossed and keeps straight at the run's own goal creature; a creature walking onto the route ahead is one replan and the body passes outside its disc; a warm runner with a layer allocates nothing a step.
- *Live wayfind* (the lead, 2026-10-24 18:11-18:18 UTC; `--from=0xA9B40019@84,7.7,94 --wayfind-back`): the Brawler to the belt 0xA5B00032 1,072.0 m in 112.0 s (9.57 m/s) and back 1,060.0 m in 113.9 s; the Archer to the forest 0x9FB50039 1,889.6 m in 182.6 s (10.35 m/s) and back 1,875.8 m in 183.0 s; every leg `ok=true rungs=0 stuck=0 corrections=0 where_max_m=0.00`; turns 50, 57, 54 and 57 a leg (53 to 75 key changes a minute; P5a offline 7 a second); 15 to 25 hostile creatures in view, 4 to 9 creature replans a leg, the nearest 10.8 to 11.8 m on three legs and 1.8 m on the forest return (most likely at its start in the hunt cell, among the creatures; not traced). The routes are 1 to 4 percent longer than P5a's (the detours).
- *The clip* (`cache/m4/feel/wayfinder-feel-before-after.mp4`, 23 s, 800x600, real time; frames by `--journey-shots` bursts of 50 at 0.15 s in the hero-brawler journey, 5/5 stations both times): before, a hunt seek with 20 turn keys in 76 m; after, the walk to the next cell with 14 in 212 m round a creature it had not planned for (`creature_replans=1`, 17 in view, the nearest 18.3 m).
- *Gates.* G-A (`slice-check -Stages build,test,exhaustive`): build 0 warnings, 9,020 default (+9), 732 Exhaustive; the Timing test (1 km plan) 15.45 ms alone and over its 50 ms limit under the full suite's load, as on master (54 ms). **The live gate** (`live-gate.ps1 -Tag feel1` on a6e6d3e7, every loopback class: shared files changed) **1,519 s (25.3 min)**: every step PASS but the melee journey, 8/9 with `build-verify` red on `pack-full` (the Brawler's main pack held 78 of 102 against the build's 72: the day's loot, this item's two hero-brawler clips among it; the open `TODO(m4-hunt-pack-full)`, which the fresh-characters follow-up removes); its other eight stations passed (the belt hunt, the vendor's walks of 177 to 180 m with 16 turns, the cells). The lead's one rerun of `smoke -Only journey-melee` alone after the vendor station had sold 23: **9/9 PASS** (pack 68 of 102, rungs 0, sightless 0, solo frames under 50 ms). The mage 11/11 (teleports 0, recalls 4); the archer 10/10 with `rungs=1` (a seek where another creature came within 0.6 m: most likely one that had noticed the archer and charged, `hits_taken=1`) and `sightless=5` (report-only; P5b's rounds 0 to 2: three of the five are seeks of 0 m, already in range of a target behind trees, and two reached 19.7 m of theirs with every sight line blocked, `TODO(nav-sight-no-physics)`: the object goal's arrival does not plan for sight); the frame judge, the throwaway's frames and live phase B 3 of 3 clean; leak scan secrets 0.

**The hero video step, as run (2026-10-25; branch `m4/hero`, cut from master dd197cd6; D29, D32).** One Opus 5.5 lead at high effort, no agents; the session prompt `cache/m4-hero-video-prompt.md`.
- *The method changed.* P5b J7's bursts could not give motion: a 1080p frame's save stalls the game about 0.5 s, so a "dense" burst is about two frames a second. The takes are M3's method instead (docs/hero-video.md §9): the hero journeys in an on-screen 1920x1080 borderless window, muted, captured by ffmpeg ddagrab at 60 fps; `--journey-film` (new) frames retail's chase camera per station as `--journey-shots` does and saves no frame. The camera holds a station's angle by retail's own rotation and zoom only: the hunt's side turn (the Archer -40°, the Brawler 35° at 1.8), the den fight's 35° and (new) den-travel's 35°.
- *Code* (b2c5aa16): `--journey-film`; the hero mage's den-travel steps off a lifestone within 4 m before Primary Portal Recall (`stepped_off=`, the recall home's walk shared through `TravelLeg.StepOffLifestone`; the gate's den-travel unchanged); the composer's `clip` shot (a span of a captured take, encoded through the same segments, captions, crossfades, cards, 720p and poster). Tests +4 (2 step-off, 1 option, 1 composer). G-A's default run: every project green but one timing test (`A_one_kilometre_plan_takes_low_single_digit_milliseconds`, median 105 ms) that ran beside a 1080p60 capture test, and passed alone.
- *Takes* (one Godot window at a time; the characters as the last gate left them): hero-archer 5/5 (2 kills, 4 frost arrows, `route_m=2407`), hero-brawler 5/5 (3 kills with the Staff of All Aspects, `looted=5`), hero-mage 7/7 (`den=recall`, 6 rings, 3 kills, 45 projectiles). The mage's bind had nothing to do (already bound and tied), so it cast the recall at the lifestone point and the step-off rightly did not walk (`stepped_off=no`); the frame looks out over Holtburg, not into the crystal.
- *The cut* (21.93 s, 1080p60 and 720p, no audio; `cache/video/m4-hero`): the Archer running (title), Greater Frost Arrows from the side killing a Goldenback Tusker, the Brawler's staff against an Outcast Monouga, a straight cut to its corpse looted (Chainmail Tassets), Primary Portal Recall, the den arrival, one Cassius' Ring of Fire among Tusker Slaves, the end card. The first cut (25.85 s) had three rings; the owner asked for the second ring alone and the Brawler's loot. Every creature a real spawn; held items and models checked (the den arrival's low camera shows the mage's own limbs, one model); no placeholder on screen.
