  - *Takes:* hero-archer 6/6, hero-brawler 7/7, hero-mage 10/10 (`corpses_found=16 corpses_rotted=16 corpses_left=0` in the first run, after 331 s; 0 in the next). The first mage take was lost: a 12-minute screen-region capture filmed other windows that covered the game; the mage was re-filmed with ffmpeg's `gfxcapture` (Windows Graphics Capture of the game window by its handle, `cache/m4/hero/capture2.ps1`), calibrated by the game's `--video-sync-marker`.
  - *The cut* (29.87 s, 1080p60 and 720p, no audio; `cache/video/m4-hero`, the shot list `m4-hero-shotlist.json`): the title over the Archer, Greater Frost Arrows from the side with a charging Tusker felled; the Brawler fells a Banderling Scout with its Staff of All Aspects and loots it with the inventory and paper doll open; Renald the Elder's store (hard cuts, the window is up about a second); Primary Portal Recall; the cleared den; one Cassius' Ring of Fire, a Tusker Guard rears up in the flames and falls; the re-buff after the death with Strength, Endurance, Coordination and Quickness climbing by 40 each (an edit crop, 1.67x around the mage and the page, so the numbers read; the game camera is retail's). Every creature a real spawn; held items checked.
  - *Seen live, for a session of its own (proposed to the owner):* the chase camera snaps in at each loot pick-up (archer2, 90.3 s on), a lugian drawn and gone every 1-3 frames beside the looting Archer (archer2 93.55-93.85 s), and the Brawler walking through the shop's door (`TODO(d25-server-object-host)`); none is in the cut.
  - *Spend:* the meter read 77% at the end of the second round: about 2% for the whole step.
  - *M4's end (the owner asked for the milestone to be closed and pushed):* the README brought to the milestone's end (the hero video, the fighters' D41 gear and Mana Conversion, `--journey-film`, the hero journeys, server objects not yet solid); G-A in the hero worktree: build 0 warnings, the Exhaustive tier 736, the default run 9,238 passed with one failure, `AllocationTests.The_other_struct_messages_dispatch_without_allocating` (3,824 bytes under G-A's load; 3 of 3 alone and the whole Net project green; no Net change since 4.2); the baseline 9,153 to 9,238 (the hero step's tests and the corpses verb's 79), Exhaustive 736.
  - *No text (the owner, after the second round):* the title card, captions and end card detract, so the cut has none: the composer ends a list with no `endCard` on its last shot's one-second fade to black (`Get-FilmFadeOut`), and the shots grew to fill the same 29.87 s (`m4-hero-shotlist.json`). The tests in the main checkout fail `The_leak_scan_reads_the_whole_gate_folder_and_the_log_dir_R4` (live-gate.ps1:409 replaces the worktree path before the main one, so the two read the same there; it passes in a worktree, where the leads run them) and the contended path-planner timing test.

| the testing loop | the throwaway leg's teleport frame: one gen-0 GC pause of 95-97 ms at the Holtburg arrival (process time about 5 ms) in one of three solo runs, over the §5 rule's 75 ms; the 32 MB gen-0 cap in tools/godot.ps1 did not cure it live (g4); P5a's rounds 2-4 and P5b's g1, r1 and r2 read 43 to 95 ms | `m4-throwaway-teleport-frame` | **closed in P5b** (the throwaway-frame fix 4406b1e7; P5b live gate round 3: three solo throwaway runs clean). The cause: the pause was the first collection after the in-game shell's no-GC window, whenever the window held. `EndNoGCRegion` leaves gen 0 the region's budget (512 MiB on 32 heaps), so that collection came at the next arrival and promoted everything since the build (every journey's first 57-77 ms pause too; a window that ended early read 48.7 ms). Offline twin, play-checks without the fix: the first collection 3,783 frames after the build, gen 0 561.3 MiB before, 31.4 MiB promoted, a 63.6 ms pause in a 67.6 ms frame (process 4.7 ms), which failed the judged arrival window. The fix: a held window's end leaves one gen-0 collection pending, run at the start of the next retail UI step before its clock (`RetailUiLayer.Step`; run inside `Complete` it raised play-checks' worst UI step from 6.7 to 11.8 ms of the 12 ms limit), reported as `closed by a gen0 collection of <ms>` on the first-collection line and play-checks' `shell_window_closing_gc_ms`; the slow-frame lines carry the collection's size (`gc_last_generation`, `gc_last_pause_ms`, `gc_last_gen0_before_mib`, `gc_last_promoted_mib`); the throwaway's and play-checks' frame lines read the collector at the frame's start (the throwaway's line said `gc_pause_ms=0` for the 62 ms pause); `NoGcWindowTests` pins the runtime fact and the fix. With it, play-checks (slice-check: build 0 warnings, 9,004 tests, play-checks 189/189, worst judged frame 22.9 ms): three held windows closed by 8.9-10.0 ms collections, the next natural ones 29-72 frames later at 19-60 MiB and 5.5-11.5 ms, worst UI step 6.3 ms. Play-checks' own reported teleport frame (55-73 ms at the character list after a log off, no collection in it) is another frame, not this one |
